- **factory-ui**: Factory UI/UX Agent. Implements or adjusts interface, layout, styling and interaction, then checks it in a real browser at desktop and phone sizes with screenshots. Respects existing design systems and approved visuals. ([DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA](https://github.com/DougZeThug/THE-HEDGEHOG-S-DILEMMA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-ui.md))

- **factory-ui**: Factory reviewer (ui). Reviews a job's branch by the project's ui rules (by default, correctness and test coverage against spec.md); writes review-ui.md for the round. Spawned by the /factory orchestrator only. ([mattmccray/factory](https://github.com/mattmccray/factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-ui.md))
- **factory-ui-reviewer**: Use before handing over any change to how a website looks or responds. Starts the branch and the default branch, pictures the pages the branch changed at phone and desktop widths in light and dark with `factory site compare`, and reviews the difference as a UI designer would, against the built-in rules and the repository's design guide when it has one. Also reviews a directory of mockup screenshots without building. Read-only, and slow. Not for whether a reader can find things or for test code (see factory-test-reviewer). ([davidbyoung/software-factory](https://github.com/davidbyoung/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-ui-reviewer.md))
- **factory-ux**: Activates when an approved PRD has user-facing surface and the run has no design artifact yet. Owns UX Design — flows, states, and the design-system seed under docs/design, which the second human gate approves. ([mattbutlerengineering/skills](https://github.com/mattbutlerengineering/skills/blob/HEAD/factory/agents/factory-ux.md) · ★1)
- **factory-ux**: Factory UI/UX Developer. Use for screens, components, layout and usability work. Builds the interface, then checks it in a real browser at phone and desktop width with screenshots. Respects the existing design system. ([DougZeThug/717rec](https://github.com/DougZeThug/717rec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-ux.md))
- **factory-ux-critic**: End-user perspective critic for materially changed user journeys. Spawn after verification passes and before opening the PR when a change introduces or materially changes a user-completable flow, interaction, state transition, error/recovery path, responsive layout, authentication, payment, onboarding, or destructive action — mobile (simulator via argent), web (browser tools), or Electron (argent chromium). Requires the caller to pass `worktree: &lt;absolute path&gt;` and how to launch the app. It uses the running app the way a real user would and returns ranked findings plus a ship/fix-first/not-a… ([watt-mind/factory](https://github.com/watt-mind/factory/blob/HEAD/dist/muse/agents/factory-ux-critic.md) · ★20 · 6 copies)
- **factory-verifier**: Independent verification of a factory change. Reads the diff cold, re-runs gates itself, and reaches its own verdict without trusting the implementer's account. Use after factory-implement completes and before any PR is opened. ([addyosmani/factory](https://github.com/addyosmani/factory/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/factory-verifier.md) · ★371 · 3 copies)
- **factory-verifier**: Adversarial verification of an implementation against its spec — runs the tests, tries to break it, returns a strict JSON verdict. Never modifies the code. ([NaejEL/DuneOS](https://github.com/NaejEL/DuneOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-verifier.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **factory-verifier**: Factory verifier for this repository. Independently proves or disproves that a built factory job meets its acceptance criteria by running it first-hand, then records pass/fail. Use only from the factory run loop, with a job id. ([kwcantrell/autologger-marketplace](https://github.com/kwcantrell/autologger-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-verifier**: Vérification adversariale d'une implémentation par rapport à sa spec — exécute les tests, cherche à casser, rend un verdict JSON strict. Ne modifie jamais le code. - **factory-watcher**: Software factory deploy watcher. The /factory land command spawns one per merged PR whose repo has after_merge.watch, to follow the merge commit's deploy with the deploy-watch skill, roll back a production outage it caused, and write watch.md with a DEPLOY verdict. Never merges. ([yoieh/factory](https://github.com/yoieh/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-watcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **factory-worker**: Agent Factory teammate — owns ONE Linear unit at a time end-to-end in its own worktree with strict test-first discipline (Tester-RED → Coder-GREEN → Documenter), posts proof, marks the unit Merging, then self-claims the next ready unit. Used as an agent-team teammate or a standalone subagent. ([Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf](https://github.com/Just-Another-Agent-Factory/jaf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-worker.md) · ★1)
- **factory-worker**: Swarm worker for parallel fan-out. One slice of a coverage matrix, race arm, gauntlet or exploration partition. Runs in its own worktree so parallel workers never touch each other's files. ([plicjo/factory](https://github.com/plicjo/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-worker.md))
- **factory-worker**: Executes a single UI or backend task file end-to-end. Reads the task spec, calls Ollama for code generation, writes files, runs build/tests, commits, and reports results. ([vervstack/Velez](https://github.com/vervstack/Velez/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/factory-worker.md))
- **factory-worker**: Software factory worker. The /factory orchestrator spawns one per job group to do the group's produce step (build, fix, research, write a doc, or any step in the catalogue), following the step file. In a code job it works in the job's worktree, tests and commits; in a files job it writes only under the job's out folder. It is resumed for each new round and for merge conflicts. ([yoieh/factory](https://github.com/yoieh/factory/blob/HEAD/agents/factory-worker.md) · 2 copies)
